# List of companies of Japan



Location of [Japan](15573.md)

This is a **list of notable [companies](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Company) based in [Japan](15573.md)**. For further information on the types of business entities in this country and their abbreviations, see "[Business entities in Japan](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Types_of_business_entity#Japan)". Note that **株式会社** can be (and frequently is) read both *[kabushiki kaisha](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Kabushiki_kaisha)* and *[kabushiki gaisha](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Kabushiki-gaisha)* (with or without a hyphen).

&#x20;\

## Former companies, including acquired and merged ones

- [Bandai](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Bandai) and [Namco](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Namco) - Merged to form [Bandai Namco Entertainment](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Bandai_Namco_Entertainment)

- [Data East](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Data_East) - went into Bankruptcy

- [Dream Stage Entertainment](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Dream_Stage_Entertainment)

- [Daiei Film](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Daiei_Film) - went into Bankruptcy, later became part of [Tokuma Shoten](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Tokuma_Shoten) and now part of [Kadokawa Pictures](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Kadokawa_Pictures)

- [Japanese National Railways](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Japanese_National_Railways) [日本国有鉄道](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/%E6%97%A5%E6%9C%AC%E5%9B%BD%E6%9C%89%E9%89%84%E9%81%93) - Reorganized into [Japan Railways Group](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Japan_Railways_Group)

- [Japan Post](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Japan_Post) [日本郵政公社](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/%E6%97%A5%E6%9C%AC%E9%83%B5%E6%94%BF%E5%85%AC%E7%A4%BE) - Reorganized into [Japan Post Group](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Japan_Post_Group)

- [Koei](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Koei) and [Tecmo](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Tecmo) - Merged to form [Koei Tecmo](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Koei_Tecmo)

- [Konica](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Konica) and [Minolta](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Minolta) - Merged to form [Konica Minolta](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Konica_Minolta)

- [Prince Motor](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Prince_Motor) merged into [Nissan](21240.md)

- [Spike](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Spike_\(company\)) and [Chunsoft](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Chunsoft) - Merged to form [Spike Chunsoft](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Spike_Chunsoft)

- [Square](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Square_\(video_game_company\)) and [Enix](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Enix) - Merged to form [Square Enix](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Square_Enix)

- [Daiichi Pharmaceutical Co.](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Daiichi_Pharmaceutical_Co.) and [Sankyo Co.](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Sankyo_Co.) - Merged to form [Daiichi Sankyo](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Daiichi_Sankyo)

- [Takata Corporation](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Takata_Corporation) - went bankrupt then was acquired by [Joyson Safety Systems](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Joyson_Safety_Systems)

- [Yamanouchi Pharmaceutical](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Yamanouchi_Pharmaceutical) and [Fujisawa Pharmaceutical](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Fujisawa_Pharmaceutical) - Merged to form [Astellas Pharma](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Astellas_Pharma)

- [Yaohan](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Yaohan)

- [Tomen](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Tomen?action=edit\&redlink=1) - Acquired by [Toyota Tsusho](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Toyota_Tsusho)

- [Nichimen](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Nichimen?action=edit\&redlink=1) and [Nissho Iwai](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Nissho_Iwai) - Merged to form [Sojitz](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Sojitz)
